# VLAN 561 (IND-CESMS) – Outstanding Items

## Overview

This document consolidates the review of the Central Engineering Management System network (VLAN 561, IND-CESMS, Edendale/Camps Drift, 192.168.161.x). It is built from `VLAN_561_CESMS.pdf` and compared with `VLAN_561_CESMS_old.pdf`.

The purpose is to identify devices that need attention: names that do not match, devices marked as problematic, devices that did not appear on the scan, and gaps in the hostname data. Nothing here has been verified against the devices themselves.

> [!info] How this list was organised
> 
> - **Hostname** is the document value and is a mix of hostnames, vendor names and scan notes. **Actual hostname** is what the IP scanner returned: `-` means the device has no hostname, and a blank means it could not be accessed.
> - **Not in use** (red) rows are labelled `Investigate` in the PDF. They are in the document but did not appear on the scan. This is not the same as decommissioned, because that cannot be verified.
> - Devices are included under every heading that applies. A device can be both a name mismatch and problematic.
> - `—` means the PDF does not provide a value for that field.

## Name mismatch

These are devices where the `Hostname` and `Actual hostname` values in the PDF are both filled in and do not match. The split below is based on pattern matching, so re-check any row that looks wrong. Some `Actual hostname` values are model or identity strings (for example `LINK150-70F2E4`), not names, and are marked **model string**.

## Documentation notes

> [!note] Source issues
> 
> - `192.168.160.161` in the PDF is almost certainly `192.168.161.161` (IND-IGN10). It is shown as `.161` here. In the old PDF that row had no IP at all.
> - 2 rows have content but no status.
> - `.203` is `Reserved` but shaded green like an in-use row.
> - `.0` is not listed, and `.255` is listed with no data.
> - Original spellings are preserved (`Rasbery Pie`, `Conductiving`, `Severtude`) so searches against the source still work.
> - The old PDF had `EDCS_BulkWater_PWM` in the Status column of `.69`. This is treated as a misplaced hostname and is not shown as an old status.
> - Light green on `.21` and `.31` is ignored and treated as green.
